# Phase 779 HSAI Formal Source Ledger Expansion

## Status

Complete as a documentation-first field-complete blocked ledger expansion.

State slice: `phase-779-hsai-formal-source-ledger-expansion`.

Classification: `SourceLedgerExpandedBlocked`.

Execution status: `NotRun`. Evidence ceiling: `Level1LocalReplayOrLower`.

## Verdict

Phase 779 expands all 102 Phase 778 ordinary command IDs into closed
`hsai-formal-source-ledger-v1` JSONL rows. Every required key is present and
no additional key is permitted. Exact values use `kind=resolved`; unsupported
values use nonempty blocker references.

Ordinal, operation ID, and three unique transcript paths are exact. Capability
values are declared in every row, but Phase 780 later disputes source
correspondence for four materialization rows. Phase 776 also resolves 28 native
expected outcomes and immutable Git, zstd, and tar executable roles. All other
fields remain fail-closed.

All 102 rows are `blocked`. Therefore no source-ledger SHA-256 is published,
and the document creates no executable plan or execution authority.

## Closed Field Schema

```text
Field<T> =
  {"kind":"resolved","value":T}
  | {"kind":"blocked","blocker_ids":[nonempty unique blocker IDs]}
```

Each row contains exactly:

```text
schema ordinal operation_id controlling_phase_and_anchor argv_template
cwd_template replacement_environment capability timeout_seconds
stdout_cap_bytes stderr_cap_bytes status_path_template stdout_path_template
stderr_path_template expected_reason expected_return_code expected_signal
typed_input_artifacts typed_output_artifacts acceptance_operation_id
executable_roles allowed_placeholders resolution_status blockers
```

Each blocker contains exactly:

```text
blocker_id affected_field cause source_anchor resolution_requirement
```

`source_anchor` contains `phase_id`, `document_path`, and `anchor`.
A blocked row has at least one blocker; a resolved row would have none.

## Closed Blocker Causes

```text
exact-downloader-argv-unresolved
exact-installer-argv-unresolved
helper-file-order-unresolved
native-transcript-grammar-unresolved
driver-preflight-argv-unresolved
archive-inventory-unresolved
mutable-output-inventory-unresolved
machine-executable-identity-acceptance-unresolved
fully-expanded-row-field-unresolved
```

Unknown placeholders, recursion, repeated braces, NUL, relative or noncanonical
paths, traversal, owner escape, and symlink ancestors are validation failures,
not authoring blockers.

## Mechanical Expansion Results

These are Phase 779's declared row values. Phase 780 does not accept the
capability split as source-correspondent until rows 036, 037, 059, and 060 are
resolved.

```text
row_count=102
unique_operation_id_count=102
ordinal_range=001..102
external_acquisition_count=10
sandbox_closed_count=33
host_offline_count=59
native_exact_outcome_count=28
blocked_row_count=102
resolved_row_count=0
source_ledger_digest=absent
```

The dedicated controlled-loopback lifecycle remains outside these ordinary
rows. The irreversible external-network closure remains after
`resolve-hostname-before-closure` and before the loopback lifecycle and
ordinal 070.

Machine observations such as canonical paths, symlink hops, device/inode, and
observed executable digests remain outside immutable rows. Rows bind immutable
roles and policies only where the controlling contract is already exact.

Canonical JSONL byte serialization is not yet frozen. That document-level
blocker must be closed before any future source-ledger digest, even after all
rows become resolved.

## Claim Boundary

This expansion is a blocked specification artifact. It is not a command
manifest accepted by the bounded adapter, a plan-v2 object, an executor binding,
a machine identity record, a transcript, a backend result, or evidence that any
command can execute successfully.

## Phase 780 Gate

Phase 780 must remain documentation-first. It may add one blocker-resolution
matrix plus standard mirrors. For every blocker cause, it must name exact
controlling sources, affected operation IDs and fields, required immutable
outputs, review criteria, and exit gates. It must also assign closure of
canonical JSONL serialization. It may split blocker families into later source
implementation phases, but may not resolve a field by assumption.

Phase 780 may not modify Python or Rust source, create attempt roots, resolve
machine identities, publish a source-ledger digest, or run any producer.
Plan-v2 and executor implementation remain prohibited while any ledger row is
blocked.

Phase 779 creates no executable plan, source-ledger digest, executor binding,
machine-resolved attempt, backend result, generated Lean, retained kernel
result, proof artifact, checker transcript, accepted evidence, Level2+, score
axis, semantic correctness, production readiness, SOTA, breakthrough, full
security, external audit, or action authority.

Phase 780 subsequently audits all blocker families in
`docs/780-phase-hsai-formal-blocker-resolution-matrix.md`. It finds that rows
036, 037, 059, and 060 have a capability-source correspondence discrepancy:
their Phase 779 `sandbox-closed` values lack an explicit supersession of the
earlier `host-offline` contracts. The Phase 779 JSONL remains an immutable
blocked historical artifact; its declared capability counts are not accepted
source correspondence. Phase 781 must resolve that discrepancy before any
row-expansion successor.
